Toolbar Zoom-in remains the zoomGeneration ink-commit proof; live pinch mid-ink discards. Wave 5: `fix-logs/e2e-wave-remaining.md`. Unblocked follow-up: `fix-logs/e2e-unblocked-followup.md`.
